                                                      \r\n  The setRef function in @radix-ui/react-compose-refs recursively triggers itself, causing React to hit the maximum update depth limit.               \r\n  In React 19, ref callback behavior changed - ref callbacks can now return cleanup functions, and React may call ref callbacks differently than in React 18. When setRef calls  \r\n  ref(value) at line 11, it triggers a re-render which invokes setRef again via the composed ref callback at line 22, creating an infinite loop.    \r\n\r\n\r\n### Expected behavior\r\n\r\n  Composed refs should work without causing infinite update loops in React 19.                                                                                                    \r\n\r\n### Reproducible example\r\n\r\nI have platejs plugins with radix popovers and radix tooltips on them. If I write fast inside plate, the page breaks. \r\nI\'m on a Macbook M1 Pro with 32 GB. Handle the cleanup function return value from React 19 ref callbacks       \r\n\r\n### Additional context\r\nRight now, it works fine with react 18 (even on next.js 16+) and we had to downgrade.\r\n\r\nI also saw this reddit post https://www.reddit.com/r/react/comments/1nnvwsp/react_19_causes_maximum_update_depth_exceeded/ where a user raised a similar case\r\n\r\nReact 19 introduced changes to ref callback behavior, including support for cleanup functions returned from ref callbacks. Don't fall back to treating the `Slottable` wrapper\n      // itself as the slot target — throw a descriptive error below instead.\n      !hasSlottable &&\n      React.Children.count(children) === 1 &&\n      React.isValidElement(children)\n    ) {\n      slottableElement = children;\n    }\n\n    if (!slottableElement) {\n      // Empty/falsy children (`null`, `undefined`, `false`, no children, etc.) are valid and\n      // render nothing. -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n\ntype AnyProps = Record<string, any>;\n\nfunction mergeProps(slotProps: AnyProps, childProps: return (element.props as { ref?: React.Ref<unknown> }).ref || (element as any).ref;\n}\n\n/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\ntype {'role': 'assistant', 'content': [{'type': 'text', 'text': 'Done.\n\n## Root cause\n\nThe bug report\'s infinite loop (`setRef` recursing through `composeRefs`, "Maximum update depth exceeded" in React 19) stems from `@radix-ui/react-compose-refs` not forwarding React 19\'s ref-cleanup protocol. In React 19 a callback ref may return a cleanup function; `composeRefs` must capture and re-return that cleanup so React can detach the ref. The old `composeRefs` (`refs.forEach((ref) => setRef(ref, node))`) discarded the return value, so the composed ref callback returned nothing to React. Under fast re-renders (typing in a Slate/Plate editor, as in the report) the composed ref can\'t be detached/re-attached correctly and React recurses in `setRef`, eventually throwing "Maximum update depth exceeded."\n\n## Finding: the fix is already present in this checkout\n\nThe repo\'s `packages/react/compose-refs/src/compose-refs.tsx` already contains the upstream fix (added 2024-12-12 in commit `9fca9020`): `setRef` returns `ref(value)`, and `composeRefs` collects cleanups and returns a cleanup function when any composed ref returns one. The report targets `@radix-ui/react-compose-refs@1.1.0`; this checkout ships the fixed source (version 1.1.2), byte-identical to `origin/main`. So no source change was required — the bug was already resolved upstream.\n\n## What I changed\n\n- `packages/react/slot/src/slot.test.tsx` — added a regression block **"React 19 ref callback cleanup support"** with two tests through `Slot` (which uses `composeRefs`):\n  1. A child ref callback returning a cleanup function: asserts the node attaches, attach runs exactly once (no loop), and the cleanup runs on unmount.\n  2. Ref objects attach/detach correctly through `Slot`.\n\n## Verification\n\n- I reproduced the failure against the **pre-fix** `composeRefs` (temporarily reverted the source): the new cleanup test failed — `expect(childCleanup).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1)` got `0`, because the old code discarded the child ref\'s cleanup so React never ran it on unmount.\n- Restored the fixed source and re-ran: `pnpm vitest run packages/react/slot` → **31 passed | 2 skipped** (up from 29). The 2 skipped are the pre-existing `describe.skip(\'given an Input\')` block, untouched.\n\nNet diff: only the test file changed (`packages/react/slot/src/slot.test.tsx`, +60 lines). No unrelated tests modified.'}], 'api': 'openai-completions', 'provider': 'pod-dsv4', 'model': 'deepseek-v4-flash-0731', 'usage': {'input': 48962, 'output': 591, 'cacheRead': 0, 'cacheWrite': 0, 'totalTokens': 49553, 'cost': {'input': 0, 'output': 0, 'cacheRead': 0, 'cacheWrite': 0, 'total': 0}}, 'stopReason': 'stop', 'timestamp': 1787264430354, 'responseId': 'chatcmpl-90b891360e8193df', 'duration': 2618.244800000044, 'ttft': 419.3781000000308, 'contextSnapshot': {'promptTokens': 48962, 'nonMessageTokens': 13936}}
